ash: Validate per-path access permissions on on-demand isolated URLs

Update ash::FileSystemBackend::IsAccessAllowed() to evaluate per-path
FileAccessPermissions for isolated URLs corresponding to virtual file
system types (provided, MTP, ARC) with opaque origins, rather than
allowing them unconditionally.

Update callers that mint isolated URLs to register explicit access
permissions. ExternalFileResolver now assigns a scoped grant to an
opaque origin for the requested virtual path during request processing.
Additionally, convert browser-internal callers such as
ArcFileSystemBridge to directly use external mount point FileSystemURLs
instead of registering unused isolated file systems.

TAG=agy

Extensions: Own NativeMessagingManager via JniUniquePtr

Java NativeMessagingManager owns its native NativeMessagingManager:
the constructor creates it through initialize() and destroy() deletes
it. The ownership was a raw long plus a dedicated destroy native
method, so the type system could not tell the value apart from any
other long.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeNativeMessagingManager>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed.

No behavior change: the null check guards destroy() exactly as the 0
check did, and no other native method takes the pointer.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

[Linux] Persist offered GlobalShortcuts portal shortcuts

Each BindShortcuts call replaces the set of shortcuts that the portal
has stored for the app, and some backends (GNOME, Hyprland) return an
empty list from ListShortcuts, require a bind in every session to
activate shortcuts, and show a dialog for any shortcut that isn't
stored. Since GlobalAcceleratorListenerLinux only bound the shortcuts
of loaded profiles, starting the browser with a different profile
overwrote the stored set and showed the dialog again on the next start.

Persist every global shortcut that has been offered, with whether the
portal has accepted it, in local state via a new
GlobalAcceleratorListener::StateStore, and include the shortcuts of
profiles that aren't loaded in every bind so they stay stored in the
portal.

81e526ba4b46fcc7f181c1f5daaf9db1711add6d [ diff ] 
[OptimizationGuide] Prevent duplicate Profile observation in ChromeProfileDownloadServiceTracker

When `LazyKeyedServiceInstantiation` defers Optimization Guide services
(including the model services deferred in https://crrev.com/c/8498635),
`OptimizationGuideGlobalState` and its owned
`ChromeProfileDownloadServiceTracker` are no longer instantiated during
`BrowserContextDependencyManager::CreateBrowserContextServices()` on
ChromeOS login, and are instead lazily instantiated for the first time
from within `ChromeBrowserMainParts::ProfileInitManager::OnProfileAdded`
(via `ChromeBrowserMainExtraPartsSegmentationPlatform::PostProfileInit`)
during `ProfileManager::DoFinalInit()`.

In `ProfileManager::DoFinalInit()`, `profile_info->MarkProfileAsCreated()`
is called immediately before iterating `observers_` for
`OnProfileAdded(profile)`. When `ChromeProfileDownloadServiceTracker` is
constructed during an earlier observer's `OnProfileAdded(profile)`:
1. Its constructor registers itself with `ProfileManager` (appending to
   the end of `observers_` mid-iteration) and loops over
   `profile_manager->GetLoadedProfiles()`, which already contains
   `profile`, calling `OnProfileAdded(profile)` ->
   `profile->AddObserver(this)` a first time.
2. `ProfileManager::DoFinalInit()` then reaches the newly appended
   `ChromeProfileDownloadServiceTracker` at the end of `observers_` and
   calls `OnProfileAdded(profile)` -> `profile->AddObserver(this)` a
   second time for the same profile, hitting `DUMP_WILL_BE_NOTREACHED()
   << "Observers can only be added once!"` in `base::ObserverList`.

Guard `ChromeProfileDownloadServiceTracker::OnProfileAdded()` with
`std::ranges::contains(observed_profiles_, profile)` (matching the
existing pattern in `SegmentationPlatformProfileObserver::OnProfileAdded`)
so the same profile is not observed twice.

TAG=agy
CONV=ad13cbf0-46d3-4ed8-b43d-7692b3e576d8

[Cast MRP] Add unit tests for joining Cast SDK sessions

CastActivityManager::JoinSession had no test coverage. The Cast Web
Sender SDK relies on it to reconnect to an existing session by session
ID ("cast-session_<id>") and to auto-join sessions according to the
page's autoJoinPolicy. The only end-to-end coverage of these flows lived
in google3 and has not run since 2020; it is being deleted
(b/500613219).

This adds CastActivityManagerJoinTest, which uses app activities that
track connected clients so that the join and auto-join logic runs for
real. It covers:
- Joining by session ID, including unknown session IDs, non-session
  presentation IDs, a different app, an already-connected client, and a
  removed sink.
- Auto-join for the tab_and_origin_scoped, origin_scoped and page_scoped
  policies, including mismatched tab, origin, and app.

NTP: Own MostVisitedSitesBridge via JniUniquePtr
📋 Metadata
commit
65899b0926dbe5f361d051a0ebee7a11c89d01e4
[ log ]
author

tree
7633170f20badd44c6d287beab77525d08261625
parent
b712c06d745844084a4eff96f630e87ebfa92f0f [ diff ] 
NTP: Own MostVisitedSitesBridge via JniUniquePtr

Java MostVisitedSitesBridge owns its native MostVisitedSitesBridge:
init() creates it and destroy() deletes it. The ownership was a raw
long plus a dedicated destroy native method, so the type system could
not tell the value apart from any other long, and every native method
accepted an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeMostVisitedSitesBridge>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed and the
destructor becomes public. Native methods take
JniPtr<NativeMostVisitedSitesBridge>, which native receives as
MostVisitedSitesBridge*.

No behavior change for correct callers: the null checks guard native
calls exactly as the 0 checks did. destroy() keeps its assert; in
release builds a second call now throws NullPointerException instead of
running Destroy() on a null native pointer.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

e5848b24a56399cf43ac41e43d07d5b4de5abe8a [ diff ] 
Page Info: Own ConnectionSecurityControllerAndroid via JniUniquePtr

Java PageInfoConnectionSecurityController owns its native
ConnectionSecurityControllerAndroid: init() creates it and destroy()
deletes it. The ownership was a raw long plus a dedicated destroy native
method, so the type system could not tell the value apart from any other
long, and every native method accepted an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeConnectionSecurityControllerAndroid>.
Destroying the handle deletes the native object through the deleter
bound by jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed.
Native methods take JniPtr<NativeConnectionSecurityControllerAndroid>,
which native receives as ConnectionSecurityControllerAndroid*.

No behavior change: the null checks guard destroy() and every native
call exactly as the 0 checks did.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

96d91ad5cbab513c58de19129a0d43c023b4af2c [ diff ] 
Recent Tabs: Own RecentTabsPagePrefs via JniUniquePtr

Java RecentTabsPagePrefs owns its native RecentTabsPagePrefs: init()
creates it and destroy() deletes it. The ownership was a raw long plus
a dedicated destroy native method, so the type system could not tell
the value apart from any other long, and every native method accepted
an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeRecentTabsPagePrefs>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed and the
destructor becomes public. Native methods take
JniPtr<NativeRecentTabsPagePrefs>, which native receives as
RecentTabsPagePrefs*.

No behavior change for correct callers. A call after destroy() still
reaches native with a null pointer, as before. destroy() keeps its
assert; in release builds a second call now throws NullPointerException
instead of running Destroy() on a null native pointer.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

65c3488421b49d936994e5926b0e4ddb431be6a7 [ diff ] 
[GMC] Move focus into the Cast device list when it is opened

In the updated Global Media Controls UI, pressing the "Show device
list" button shows the Cast device list, but keyboard focus stays on
the button. Keyboard and screen reader users must tab through the rest
of the media item to reach the list (WCAG 2.4.3 Focus Order).

CastDeviceSelectorView now:
- Focuses the first device when the list is shown, or the close button
  if only the permission error is shown. This matches
  MediaItemUIDeviceSelectorView.
- When the list is shown before any devices are available, e.g. when
  the dialog is opened by a website through the Presentation API,
  focuses the first device on the first device update. This is skipped
  if another view already has focus, so focus is not taken from the
  user.
- Returns focus to the start casting button when the list is hidden,
  after the button's accessible name is updated, so focus is not left
  on a hidden view.
- Keeps focus on the same device when the list is rebuilt, and moves
  focus to a visible view when the device list and the permission error
  replace each other, or when the device list becomes empty.

Like MediaItemUIDeviceSelectorView, focus also moves when the list is
opened with a mouse click.

Added unit tests for where focus goes when the list opens or closes,
when devices arrive after the list is shown, when the device list
refreshes or becomes empty, and when the device list and the
permission error replace each other.

Verified with Narrator on Windows. The Presentation API case was
verified with a local browser test that uses the test media route
provider.

📋 Metadata

1e08c0ce4bebc02a4e6533b0390db5b8b116bf71
parent
197394d6f30baf97ac2c3b4fd888c44d7eec3ef6 [ diff ] 
🌺 Give saved pages unique titles in OfflinePage instrumentation tests

On Android Q+, saving an offline page publishes it into the MediaStore
Downloads collection under the page title (DISPLAY_NAME =
page.getTitle()). Every test saves the same test page, titled "About".

android-code-coverage runs these tests on reused physical Pixel 2
devices whose /sdcard/Download is never wiped. Leftovers from earlier
runs (About, About (1), About (2), ...) make MediaProvider's rename fail:

  MediaProvider: Moving .../Download/About (1) to .../Download/About (2)
  E DatabaseUtils: SQLiteConstraintException: UNIQUE constraint failed:
      files._data
  E cr_OPArchivePublisher: Failed to finish publishing archive.

publishArchiveToDownloadsCollection then returns an empty path, which
is reported as SavePageResult::FILE_MOVE_FAILED (12). About 10
OfflinePageBridgeTest/OfflinePageUtilsTest methods fail this way, in
up to 24/30 builds:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-code-coverage/8160

The leftovers come from app-data wipes (reinstalls, state cleared after
failed tests), which a per-test teardown cannot clean up. Instead,
give the page a unique document.title before each save so the
published name can never collide.

Bug: 575823388

[Contextual Tasks] Match tab picker font to Search menu

The Search page embeds Chrome's tab picker via iframe as the "Add tabs"
row of its (+) menu. The tab picker row from Chrome rendered in Google
Sans, loaded from fonts.googleapis.com, while the AIM page's rows render
in Arial. This caused a mismatch.

Fix: Use Arial for the trigger row, the flyout items and the "Recent"
suffix so the row matches its neighbors. CSS doesn't inherit into the
iframe, so must hard code.

This also removes the Google Sans stylesheet and the host permissions
and CSP directives that were added only to load it, restoring the
manifest to its state before https://crrev.com/c/8485586.

Stop using LSan use_unaligned=1 on non-Fuchsia platforms

b2d11a7ec4314f49ae4dfda1c5daef67cbe12583

Wed Oct 21 01:59:12 2026
committer

Wed Oct 21 01:59:12 2026
tree
24bc3424d847bf3295d9fac9f4600cc5693ecff8
parent
4e597b756e68c187fa3dac614ed868bfae794ab7 [ diff ] 
Stop using LSan use_unaligned=1 on non-Fuchsia platforms

Only pass use_unaligned=1 for Fuchsia 64-bit LSan builds. Exempt an
unaligned pointer in //base/substring_set_matcher/ from LSan.

The goal is to avoid the expensive unaligned memory scan in LSan on
shutdown - measurements show it might be causing a 10-30% slowdown in
browser_tests. The "use_unaligned=1" LSan option was originally added
for V8 in 2019 in crrev.com/c/1926472 to let LSan see the unaligned raw
pointers in the V8 heap. Since then V8 implemented the Sandbox that
avoids storing any raw pointers on the JS heap; the Sandbox is now
enabled in all Chromium 64-bit builds except for Fuchsia.

The change in //base/substring_set_matcher/ is a workaround since the
Aho-Corasick code is using `__attribute__((packed))` and hence triggers
spurious LSan leak reports in the default configuration.

90e91596dc5f8547edbd273526ce81fc6c5ac54f
parent
f41786db4eeb5f1a8cd6005071313f681dc04155 [ diff ] 
Toolbar: Own LocationBarModelAndroid via JniUniquePtr

Java LocationBarModel owns its native LocationBarModelAndroid:
initializeWithNative() creates it and destroy() deletes it. The
ownership was a raw long plus a dedicated destroy native method, so the
type system could not tell the value apart from any other long, and
every native method accepted an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeLocationBarModelAndroid>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method and
LocationBarModelAndroid::Destroy() are removed. Native methods take
JniPtr<NativeLocationBarModelAndroid>, which native receives as
LocationBarModelAndroid*.

No behavior change: the null checks guard destroy() and every native
call exactly as the 0 checks did.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

[KeyboardAccessory] Short-circuit isFillingViewShown

When ChromeKeyboardVisibilityDelegate.isKeyboardShowing checks whether
the keyboard is visible while the soft keyboard is closed,
isSoftKeyboardShowing queries window insets to calculate the keyboard
height and returns false, and then calls
ManualFillingComponent.isFillingViewShown.

Previously, ManualFillingMediator.isFillingViewShown called
!isSoftKeyboardShowing(view) before checking whether the accessory has
an active tab or is in a sheet state, causing a second redundant
keyboard height calculation on every check when no filling view is
shown.

Reorder the conditions in ManualFillingMediator.isFillingViewShown to
evaluate the in-memory accessory and extension state before calling
!isSoftKeyboardShowing(view). Local testing reveals it can save up to
12ms on the main thread during startup.

Bug: b:575418187
Test: out/Default/bin/run_keyboard_accessory_junit_tests

bb3dba21839150107ae26c81c0c2ebedbea1c1ef
parent
902c4623b0642145ebf0e9e9cac2a3e82fb4026c [ diff ] 
Content settings: Own AndroidObserver via JniUniquePtr

Java ContentSettingsObserver owns its native
content_settings::AndroidObserver: init() creates it and destroy()
deletes it. The ownership was a raw long plus a dedicated destroy native
method, so the type system could not tell the value apart from any other
long.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeAndroidObserver>. Destroying the handle
deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed and the
AndroidObserver destructor becomes public.

No behavior change for correct callers. No native method other than
destroy takes the pointer. destroy() keeps its assert; in release builds
a second call is now a no-op instead of a double delete.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

Content settings: Own CookieControlsBridge via JniUniquePtr

Java CookieControlsBridge owns its native CookieControlsBridge: the
constructor creates it through init() and destroy() deletes it. The
ownership was a raw long plus a dedicated destroy native method, so
the type system could not tell the value apart from any other long,
and every native method accepted an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeCookieControlsBridge>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed. Native
methods take JniPtr<NativeCookieControlsBridge>, which native receives
as CookieControlsBridge*.

No behavior change: the null checks guard destroy() and every native
call exactly as the 0 checks did.

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

android-binary-size: Measure only Chrome (not WebView) for arm64

In order to speed up the trybot.

This changes the bot to measure WebView only for arm32:
* The arm64 size config / .ssargs (now "Chrome64") and the 64-bit
  resource_sizes inputs contain only Chrome.
* check_webview_static_initializers checks only the 32-bit library.
* The trybot builds chrome_public_minimal_apks rather than
  chrome_and_webview_64_minimal_apks, so it now links libchrome.so
  (arm + arm64) and libwebviewchromium.so (arm) only.

chrome_and_webview_64_minimal_apks and ChromeAndWebView64.ssargs are
kept for android-desktop-*-binary-size and diagnose_bloat.py.

Search Engines: Own ExtensionControlHandler via JniUniquePtr

Java ExtensionControlHandler owns its native ExtensionControlHandler:
init() creates it and destroy() deletes it. The ownership was a raw
long plus a dedicated destroy native method, so the type system could
not tell the value apart from any other long, and every native method
accepted an arbitrary address.

Hold it as JniUniquePtr<NativeExtensionControlHandler>. Destroying the
handle deletes the native object through the deleter bound by
jni_zero::MakeUnique, so the destroy native method is removed.
disableExtension() takes JniPtr<NativeExtensionControlHandler>, which
native receives as ExtensionControlHandler*.

No behavior change. destroy() keeps its null check, so a second call is
still a no-op. disableExtension() keeps its assert; in release builds a
call after destroy() still reaches native with a null pointer, as
before.

746c03c617e278d0c1e219f52a6b1cb27bd18e2a
parent
481ca0526efdc37427be0b75b190b4a1a5122c78 [ diff ] 
Ash: Migrate RemotingClientIOProxyImplTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

RemotingClientIOProxyImplTest already waits for the first session's
session-ended notification before checking that the replacement session
has not ended. The saved callback posts to the original observer, which
completes the existing TestFuture wait. Remove the redundant idle drain
while keeping both session assertions intact.

[CrOS] Avoid loading TTS engines when indexing settings search

AccessibilitySection::UpdateTextToSpeechVoiceSearchTags() called
TtsController::GetVoices() at login just to check !voices.empty().
However, GetVoices() unconditionally calls LoadBuiltInTtsEngine(),
loading and waking the built-in Google TTS and eSpeak extensions and
spawning TtsService (~102 MiB PSS at login).

Since ChromeOS always includes built-in TTS voices, register the voice
search tags directly without calling GetVoices().

updated automatically. Crowbar requires upstream files of a
third-party dependency to live in a src/ directory, with Chromium glue
next to it.

  are pure renames; libspeechd.h only changes the path in its
  existing Chromium #include of speechd_types.h.

- content/browser/speech: the two #includes and their DEPS rules.
- build/check_gn_headers_allowlist.txt and README.chromium (License
  File): the new paths.

2fe730be5894998056de4a13e7e9cbf5d295ac36 [ diff ] 
[WebNN] CoreML: Use identity op for no-op transpose permutations

When a transpose operation with rank >= 2 uses an identity permutation
(such as [0, 1]) on a constant input tensor, BNNS graph compilation
optimizes away the no-op transpose and crashes during model load. Extend
the existing rank <= 1 identity fast-path in AddOperationForTranspose to
emit an identity operation whenever the permutation is an identity
permutation.

bd928a0c4bc43beb1f57c3389b49f0d7e0efc02c
parent
b1c378ac11a6ee8e9f911a964f05f0ab4891270d [ diff ] 
[initial-letter] Use the computed sink for drop and an omitted sink

Per css-inline-3, `drop` computes to a sink equal to the initial letter
size floored, and an omitted sink means `drop`[1]. So `2.51` and
`2.51 drop` mean `2.51 2`: as 2.51 >= 2, the letter is under-aligned
with its baseline on line 2, and the text moves down 0.51 line.
StyleInitialLetter already stores floor(size), but layout ignored it
for these sink types and used ceil(size) instead, so `2.51` rendered
like `2.51 3`: top-aligned with no text shift.

Use Sink() for every sink type. Such letters now take the size >= sink
path and are under-aligned on the floored line. A size below 2, e.g.
`1.5`, floors to 1 and becomes a raised initial. Integer sizes do not
change. The change is guarded by the InitialLetterBlockPositioning
flag.

[1] https://drafts.csswg.org/css-inline-3/#valdef-initial-letter-drop

Tests:
- external/wpt/css/css-inline/initial-letter/initial-letter-block-position-fractional-drop.html (new)
- external/wpt/css/css-inline/initial-letter/initial-letter-block-position-fractional-drop-vrl.html (new)
- external/wpt/css/css-inline/initial-letter/initial-letter-block-position-fractional-drop-vlr.html (new)

d835a8c330bb6aa52ead818986c9fe2212c7480b
parent
51602f3c18392d7fe76d12f22c23040146bd6270 [ diff ] 
[NTP] Add NTP extension page with <ntp-logo> and <ntp-searchbox>

Replaces the NTP component extension's placeholder page with a
minimal NTP consisting of the logo/doodle (<ntp-logo>) and the realbox
(<ntp-searchbox>), reused from chrome://new-tab-page.

Extension pages can't load chrome://new-tab-page, so the NTP modules the
extension imports (as /ntp/<path>), and the NTP modules they depend on,
are packaged into the extension and served from its own origin under
/ntp/.

In unoptimized builds, a packaging script finds them by
following the imports, copies them, and rewrites scheme-relative
//resources/ URLs to chrome://resources/ so they don't resolve against
the extension's origin.

In optimized builds, they are bundled into the extension's app.js from
the NTP's compiled output instead. The NTP itself is unchanged. Icons
render regardless of the host document, as <ntp-logo> and
<ntp-doodle-share-dialog> use an iconset and load the Google logo from
chrome://resources.

The page sets <base href="chrome://resources/"> so that scheme-relative
//resources/ URLs, used both by the shared WebUI components and by the
data their browser-side handlers send, resolve as they do on WebUI
pages.

c2ce6a4d824d56f3f2feed1d73a2aa1bd60e8f12
parent
97c1c405c1f1ab05b585be8b8e19b04b78ea857f [ diff ] 
[ash] Use BrowserContext instead of Profile in three more ash directories

Each of these derived a Profile only to reach something that already
takes a content::BrowserContext:

- ImageDownloaderImpl resolved the account's browser context and converted
  it to a Profile for GetURLLoaderFactory(). It now keeps the browser
  context.
- ChromeFocusModeDelegate did the same when building the YouTube Music
  request sender. Its profile_manager.h include was unused and goes too.
- DlpInternalsUI passed Profile::FromWebUI() to
  content::WebUIDataSource::CreateAndAdd() and called IsOffTheRecord() on
  it; both are browser context operations, so it uses the WebContents'
  browser context. The Profile it handed to DlpInternalsPageHandler was
  only stored and DCHECKed, never read, so that constructor parameter and
  the member go away.

image_downloader and dlp_internals lose their //chrome/browser/profiles
allowance; focus_mode keeps one scoped to its browsertest. Three build
dependencies go, including a //chrome/browser/ash/profiles in focus_mode
that nothing there included.

#ai-assisted

tree
50e691021ef0ba0df114da6e5babaad758112c19
parent
b9b7f3d00570bba6c9027f0d9ebe661303f8fb30 [ diff ] 
autofill: Migrate ThemeTrackingImageView callbacks to ui::ColorId

This CL simplifies the background color callbacks that
`TitleWithIconAfterLabelView`, `BnplIssuerView` and
`FilledCardInformationBubbleViews` pass to
`views::ThemeTrackingImageView`. Previously, each callback resolved
`ui::kColorDialogBackground` through the parent view's
`GetColorProvider()`.

Following crrev.com/c/6241873, the callbacks return a
`ui::ColorVariant`, which `views::ThemeTrackingImageView` resolves
against its own color provider in the same widget. The callbacks can
therefore return the `ui::ColorId` directly and no longer bind `this`.

TAG=agy
CONV=d2dcde16-dfba-4397-aa8a-44c55aed547d

Screenshot: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/9uU5PKRB4Vup99N

📋 Metadata

40354f5ccf36771d40ddeca904c3fdd439e0ae88
[ log ]
author

Wed Oct 21 00:20:58 2026
tree
98d96242ab02c8aa585b803b220c664b9b545993
parent
a7748a22633d4881ec5b93d1ea252db4cc18645d [ diff ] 
Only re-arm the fullscreen bubble re-show for tab-initiated modes

crrev.com/c/8414491 made ExclusiveAccessBubbleViews::Hide() re-arm a
re-show on the next user interaction whenever a menu is open, on the
theory that the menu may have covered the exit instruction. Unlike the
menu-dismissal half of that change, this was not gated on tab-initiated
fullscreen, contrary to the CL description.

As a result, in browser fullscreen (notably on macOS, where the bubble
appears after the AppKit transition), opening the app menu while the
bubble is still showing causes the exit instruction to unexpectedly
reappear on the next interaction, e.g. after toggling vertical tabs.

Gate the re-show on IsTabFullscreenType(), matching
DismissMenusObscuringBubble(). Browser- and extension-initiated
fullscreen are user-initiated, so the re-show adds noise without a
security benefit.

Bug: 574452272

Original change's description:
> [Extensions] Implement runtime.getDocumentId() API
>
> This CL implements the synchronous chrome.runtime.getDocumentId()
> extension API which allows extension contexts and content scripts to
> synchronously retrieve the unique document ID of a given WindowProxy or
> frame owner element (iframe, frame, embed, or object), and throws a
> synchronous error if the target is invalid or detached.
>
> To support synchronous renderer-side execution, this CL:
> - Flag-guards this API logic behind a kApiRuntimeGetDocumentId flag
> - Exposes content::RenderFrameHost::GetDocumentToken() and unifies
> ExtensionApiFrameIdMap. Previously,
> ExtensionApiFrameIdMap::ExtensionDocumentUserData created a separate
> random base::UnguessableToken in the browser process that the renderer
> had no knowledge of. Now, we stop generating a separate document ID in
> the browser process and instead just use the existing document token in
> the renderer which ensures the ID will be returned synchronously.
> - Adds blink::WebFrame::FromWindow(): Blink already resolves DOM frame owner elements to a WebFrame*, but it lacked a helper to resolve a V8 Window / WindowProxy value to its corresponding WebFrame*.
> - Implements runtime.getDocumentId() in RuntimeHooksDelegate: The
> schema definition and feature rules are added and
> RuntimeHooksDelegate::HandleGetDocumentId() is implemented.
>
> Cross-origin same-process frames are supported in this CL, but
> out-of-process frames are not supported yet. This will be done in a
> separate CL.
>
> Fixed: 573738380
> Fuchsia-Binary-Size: Size increase is unavoidable.

Validate referrer in CreateNewWindowParams.

Verify in `VerifyCreateNewWindowParams()` that any non-empty referrer
URL in `mojom::CreateNewWindowParams` has an origin hosted by the
initiating renderer process according to
`ChildProcessSecurityPolicyImpl::HostsOrigin()`, matching the referrer
check in `VerifyDownloadUrlParams()`. If validation fails, terminate
the renderer process with `RFH_CREATE_NEW_WINDOW_INVALID_REFERRER` and
block window creation before any browser-side side effects occur.

link rather than following it.

parent
b763f216ad1d8aee39649a62891264f2b0cb1575 [ diff ] 
media: Guard against null scopeId in MediaDrmBridge.getKeyRequest

In removeSession(), persisting the KEY_TYPE_RELEASE key type to storage
is asynchronous. If the session's keySetId is cleared before the storage
callback runs, doRemoveSession() calls getKeyRequest() with a null
scopeId. Because the existing assert is stripped in release builds, null
is passed to MediaDrm.getKeyRequest(), throwing an unnecessary
IllegalArgumentException from JNI.

Return null early in getKeyRequest() when scopeId is null so the caller
rejects the session promise cleanly without calling into MediaDrm.

Verified MediaDrmBridgeTest on Pixel 8a.

Bug: 575561825

[remoting] Attribute Windows remote touch injection in LocalMouseInputMonitorWin

Windows touch injection (InjectTouchInput) synthesizes raw mouse events with hDevice == nullptr and ulExtraInformation set to MI_WP_SIGNATURE (0xFF515700 | pointer_id), without preserving thread-level SetMessageExtraInfo(kCrdInputExtraInfo).

This CL tracks active remote touch injection in TouchInjectorWin and input_extra_info.cc/.h (with a 150ms tail window after all contacts end) so LocalMouseInputMonitorWin distinguishes CRD-injected remote touch from local physical touch input.

TAG=agy
CONV=4320093f-c174-40f2-b212-19a4e3bea1ad

Suppress data drags during Linux frame touch-hold window moves/resizes
📋 Metadata

5491e563145bc8060a105fe599d318053194a5ea

author

tree
4a7253da1301ee4fefb552df52f6c0a64c7f5602
parent
f1cc5ecb7e06282836a434806ac7b98d25f37611 [ diff ] 
Suppress data drags during Linux frame touch-hold window moves/resizes

WindowEventFilterLinux arms a DesktopDragDropClientOzone window-move
drag suppression while the left mouse button is held on the caption, but
a touch held on the caption or a resize border armed nothing. On
Ozone/Wayland, a compromised renderer could send
LocalFrameHost::StartDragging with a touch source while the user's
finger rested on the frame, and the browser would start a
wl_data_device drag using the user's touch serial. The window would not
move or resize and lifting the finger would drop attacker-controlled
data.

Track a touch pressed on HTCAPTION or a resize border and hold a
ScopedSuppressForWindowMove scope until that touch is released or
cancelled. The scope deliberately outlives the gesture hand-off to the
compositor, since the browser keeps the touch (and its serial) live
until the compositor ends it. A new touch press, a mouse press, or
destroying the filter also releases the suppression, so it cannot be
held indefinitely if the compositor never delivers the touch release.

Fixed: 574902935

MediaDrm.getKeyRequest() only declares throws NotProvisionedException in
Java, but its JNI implementation (android_media_MediaDrm_getKeyRequest)
calls throwExceptionAsNecessary(), which throws ResourceBusyException
when the DRM HAL returns ERROR_DRM_RESOURCE_BUSY (for example, when
generating a KEY_TYPE_RELEASE request opens an internal OEMCrypto
session while all sessions are in use). Because ResourceBusyException is
a checked MediaDrmException not declared in getKeyRequest()'s throws
clause, it escapes getKeyRequest() and crashes the GPU process.

Replace catch (IllegalStateException) in getKeyRequest() with catch
(Exception) (matching getPropertyString()) so undeclared checked
MediaDrmExceptions and runtime exceptions are caught and the caller
rejects the promise cleanly.

Verified MediaDrmBridgeTests on Pixel 8a.

Bug: 575562215

Tue Oct 20 23:57:40 2026
tree
9f204ba2e9ea042edaa99f4f706a8a1b29155e42
parent
37c68dda862d49cf91e52ee01e2dc1569a414c7d [ diff ] 
Preserve float precision in MediaControlTimelineElement::SetPosition

MediaControlTimelineElement::SetDuration() sets the max attribute using
Element::SetFloatingPointAttribute(), which formats numbers via
String::NumberToStringEcmaScript(). However, SetPosition() previously
used String::Number(double), which rounds to 6 significant digits.

When current_time equals duration with more than 6 significant digits
(e.g., 6.029932s), value was truncated to "6.02993" while max remained
"6.029932", causing the slider thumb layout calculation to fall slightly
short of 100% width at the end of playback.

Use String::NumberToStringEcmaScript() in SetPosition() so value and max
have matching precision.

bb8728c63e1382c948850fe560609499f40ddb8c
parent
66eda6d95387e1ebbb1c9cd45eb53b76c8297b4b [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Avoid redundant per-property work in BaseSuggestionViewBinder

PropertyModelChangeProcessor calls BaseSuggestionViewBinder.bind() once
per PropertyKey (~28x per row per update). Previously, several expensive
operations ran unconditionally or re-applied unchanged state on every
bind:

1. Bind suggestion dimensions once on RESOURCE_PROVIDER instead of
   calling view.setSuggestionDimensions(...) on every property key.
2. Dispatch to ActionChipsBinder.bind() and bindContent() only for keys
   they own (and ignore item-decoration keys in BaseSuggestionViewBinder).
3. In updateColorScheme(), avoid calling updateSuggestionIcon() (which
   was setting the decoration icon twice per bind) and only update
   decoration/action icon tints when the ColorStateList changes. Cache
   primary and secondary icon tint ColorStateLists in
   OmniboxResourceProvider via ResourceCache.
4. Early-return when unchanged in ActionChipsView.setLeadInSpacing(),
   SuggestionLayout.applySideSpacing(),
   BaseSuggestionView.setUseLargeDecorationIcon(),
   BaseSuggestionViewBinder.bindTopPadding(), and setMinimumHeight() in
   SuggestionViewViewBinder.
5. Cache line_1 and line_2 TextView references on the content view tag
   in SuggestionViewViewBinder instead of calling findViewById() per key.
6. Reuse a single AccessibilityDelegate and OnClickListener per
   ActionButtonView reading the current Action from the view tag, and
   only call TooltipCompat.setTooltipText() when the description changes.

This reduces the cost of bind() calls by ~63%

Bug: 575958156

9ea3f075f6b37cf0e852a92c4627602eace2008d
parent
d05b51c6c4ab3cd63e8b8f7dca62f058783e8be6 [ diff ] 
Wait for latest result in ObserverNotCalledAfterRemoval

In DistillablePageUtilsBrowserTestAllArticles.
ObserverNotCalledAfterRemoval, after removing the observer,
NavigateAndWait(kSimpleArticlePath, kWaitNoExpectedCall) waits a fixed 1
second after WaitForLoadStop() to verify the removed observer is not
called, and then immediately asserts that GetLatestResult(web_contents_)
is populated. On slower builds (such as DCHECK or MSAN builds under
load), renderer distillability extraction can take longer than 1 second
after load stop, leaving GetLatestResult as nullopt when the 1-second
timer expires.

Wait using base::test::RunUntil for GetLatestResult(web_contents_) to
have a final result before asserting its value.

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3ADistillablePageUtilsBrowserTestAllArticles%23ObserverNotCalledAfterRemoval

[CrOS] Remove obsolete M99 ZeroconfScannerDetector metrics listener

CupsPrintersManagerImpl unconditionally creates a duplicate
ZeroconfScannerDetector at user login solely to record the
Scanning.NumDetectedScannersAtLogin UMA histogram after 5 minutes
(marked with a TODO(b/192467856) to remove by M99). Because
LorgnetteScannerManagerFactory already creates a
ZeroconfScannerDetector at login, this starts duplicate mDNS device
listers for three scanner service types (_uscans._tcp.local,
_uscan._tcp.local, _scanner._tcp.local) and retains a duplicate
detector and discovered scanner map in memory for 5 minutes.

Remove this obsolete metrics-only detector and histogram, and drop
the now-unused circular include dependency from
//chrome/browser/ash/printing to //chrome/browser/ash/scanning.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Scanning.NumDetectedScannersAtLogin]=Removed obsolete metric scheduled for removal in M99 (b/192467856).

DisplayAlignmentIndicator, and the kDisplayAlignAssist feature flag.

[context-input] Gate Lens requests on IsGoogleChromeAPIKeyUsed()

Gate FetchClusterInfo() and StartFileUploadFlow() in
ComposeboxQueryController on google_apis::IsGoogleChromeAPIKeyUsed() so
that unbranded developer builds with src-internal continue to work while
builds without official Chrome API keys skip LFE cluster info fetches
and immediately fail file uploads instead of hanging in kProcessing.

Also bypass the API key check in TestComposeboxQueryController,
ChromeTestSuite, and Lens browser test fixtures so existing unit and
browser tests continue to run on unbranded CQ bots.

58270884a88a120cc3e030e215b653598a857777
parent
20d34d775c96a75777d4351e92d65334cfd32e4d [ diff ] 
views: guard MenuController hot-tracked button paths against AX re-entrancy

When accessibility events such as SetActiveDescendant or kSelection are
fired synchronously during menu selection and button hot-tracking,
handlers can re-entrantly close the menu and destroy the MenuController
or the hot-tracked Button.

Add ScopedDeletionGuard and ViewTracker checks in SetHotTrackedButton,
IncrementSelection, SendAcceleratorToHotTrackedView, CloseSubmenu,
AcceptOrSelect, and SelectByChar to prevent use-after-free when the
controller or button is destroyed during accessibility notifications.

Tue Oct 20 23:27:54 2026
tree
a581df0c3c0fc54e7ad32b19b490673e247a8d59
parent
acb3f5fffbb3126cea1d50a67384279a3b640ff3 [ diff ] 
Reland: Wait for ScreenTime to be ready in WebTestSuite to prevent test flake

Waits for the simulator's `ScreenTimeAgent` and (on iOS 27+)
`ScreenTimeSettingsAgent` daemons to finish post-boot initialization and
respond to configuration requests before running tests. Otherwise,
`WKWebView` frame commits trigger synchronous `ScreenTime` XPC calls on
the Swift Concurrency cooperative thread pool that contend with
`BrowserEngineKit` web content process launch on cold-booted simulators.

Without this wait, WebKit exhausts resources waiting for ScreenTime
initialization to start leading to flaky test failures. This was
especially noticeable when attempting to add new tests in
`ios_web_inttests` triggering the new flaky test detection logic and
blocking CLs from landing unless the `Validate-Test-Flakiness: skip`
footer was added.

Changes in reland: Use LOG_IF instead of DLOG_IF to always log the
message, even when running tests in release and to prevent an unused
variable error.

[Contextual Tasks] Enable UI in rearchitecture

When `kContextualTasksSidePanelRearchitecture` is enabled without
`kContextualTasks`, Chrome hosts the Contextual Tasks toolbar WebUI and
post-rearchitecture controller while `IsContextualTasksUIEnabled()`
still reports the UI as disabled. Including
`kContextualTasksSidePanelRearchitecture` in
`IsContextualTasksUIEnabled()` ensures WebUI Mojo binders and UI
eligibility checks recognize the UI as enabled under rearchitecture.

Enabling the UI under the rearchitecture flag also activates navigation
throttling and cookie-jar avatar lookups in rearchitecture unit tests
that previously bypassed those paths. Guard `IsAllowedHost()` against a
null `AimEligibilityService`, mock `HandleNavigation` in coordinator
tests, scope side-panel `WebContents` overrides in URL interception
tests so clobbered tabs are not re-intercepted, and configure
`ChromeSigninClient` with a test URL loader for ChromeOS host tests.

c9fd66ecc528a627ec8af97f2d60f5c57e53aa0c [ diff ] 
CSP: Enforce Trusted Types in embedded CSP

Include Trusted Types directives when checking whether a response
satisfies an iframe's required CSP. Combine enforcing response policies
before conservatively comparing normalized Trusted Types restrictions,
and ignore report-only policies.

The initial trusted-types implementation compares normalized enforcement
configurations using exact equality. This is conservative and may reject
stricter returned subsets while richer subsumption is deferred.

Flag: CSPEmbeddedEnforcementTrustedTypes (disabled by default) When
disabled, CSP:EE preserves its existing source-list-only subsumption
behavior and skips Trusted Types checks.
- Chrome status entry: https://chromestatus.com/feature/5199775795380224
- I2P: https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/blink-dev/c/H-i9_ZsT6CU/m/p9t7nrn1AwAJ

- Spec issue: https://github.com/w3c/webappsec-csp/issues/628
- Spec PR: https://github.com/w3c/webappsec-cspee/pull/36

[omnibox] Make clicks beside the input not corrupt the elided URL
📋 Metadata

77464f5014d28d07288403e8368f28424feda475

Tue Oct 20 23:14:01 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Oct 20 23:14:01 2026
tree
4495fcfcd9ecb2366451c15c5ae3ac198c5a2890
parent
43944d3f7ade7539c549c255d84f415412bce046 [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Make clicks beside the input not corrupt the elided URL

In the full WebUI omnibox, with all of an elided URL (e.g.
"google.com/search?q=..." for "https://www.google.com/search?q=...")
selected, a click inside the input row but outside the <input> (e.g. in
the 18px gap left at the end of the row when the AIM button is hidden,
or on the leading icon) did nothing visible, and the next keystroke
then replaced all but the last 12 characters (the length of the elided
"https://www.") of the unelided URL.

Root cause: onInputMousedown_() is bound to all of <cr-searchbox-input>,
so it also ran for these clicks. They aren't on the input, so they took
the "deselect" branch, and showFullUrlOnDeselect_() unelided the URL.
That relies on Blink then placing the caret at the click position, which
it does for presses on the input. But OmniboxFullAppElement prevents the
default action of presses on non-focusable targets (to keep the input
focused), so nothing replaced the selection: setInputText() had kept
its numeric offsets [0, elidedLength), which on the longer unelided text
cover all but its last 12 characters. The next keystroke replaced that
stale range.

Fix:
- Only deselect (and so unelide) for presses on the input itself. A
  press elsewhere in the row now leaves the elided URL and its selection
  as is, so typing replaces all of it.
- For parity with the Views omnibox, where a click in the space after
  the text places the caret, make the trailing gap part of the input's
  hit area: instead of a margin on #mirrorContainer (added by
  crrev.com/c/8593099), pad both the input and #mirror by 18px, so they
  keep the same box, text area and max scroll offset. URLs force
  dir="ltr" on both, so the padding uses physical sides keyed off the UI
  direction, keeping the gap at the end of the field in RTL UIs. The
  "mirror-container" part is no longer needed and is removed.
  A click in the gap now unelides the URL with the caret at its end,
  like a click past the end of the text.

Clicks on the leading icon still don't place the caret (in Views, the
location icon opens page info instead); this only fixes the corruption
for them.

Test: New OmniboxPopupSearchboxTest tests:
- SecondClickOnAllSelectedElidedUrl.ClickBesideInputDoesNotCorruptText
  OnNextKeystroke presses on the row behind the (pointer-events: none)
  icon, then types with execCommand('insertText'), and checks that the
  URL stays elided and selected and is fully replaced.
- SecondClickOnAllSelectedElidedUrl.UnelidesOnClickInTrailingGapPlacing
  CaretAtEnd hit-tests the gap, which must be the input, and clicks it.
- TrailingGapIsPartOfInputWhenAimButtonHidden_{LTR,RTL} hit-tests every
  pixel of the gap with a scrolled long URL, and checks that the gap is
  on the end side in both UI directions.
assertMirrorAlignedWithInput() now compares the padding and max scroll
offsets of the input and #mirror instead of their clientWidths, since an
<input>'s clientWidth excludes its padding while a <div>'s includes it.
The existing MirrorAlignsWithScrolledInput* tests keep passing. Without
this fix, all 4 new tests fail.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
webview: Remove special handling in IsHandledURL for file urls
📋 Metadata
commit
43944d3f7ade7539c549c255d84f415412bce046
[ log ]
author
Rupert Wiser <bewise@chromium.org>
Tue Oct 20 23:13:25 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Oct 20 23:13:25 2026
tree
b8b312be0342794cacf0d61b1262485fb215f940
parent
508f04db06dd5075c58dd25e95d2e4db4f2608a1 [ diff ] 
webview: Remove special handling in IsHandledURL for file urls

When content calls IsHandledURL, we are basically telling it whether
this is a URL the browser handles or not. If we say no, content assumes
the URL is headed for some external app and lets the renderer request it
without checking permissions.

We would explicitly say no for the special file:///android_asset/ and
file:///android_res/ URLs. The idea was to create a carve out so that a
renderer that hadn't been granted the file: scheme could still navigate
to them.

However, this meant the browser never checked who was asking. Only Blink
did. So a compromised renderer on any origin could navigate to a special
file URL, and because committing that grants the process the file:
scheme, it could then navigate on to any file URL it liked.

It turns out we didn't need the carve out at all. Everything that
legitimately navigates to a special file URL already has the file:
grant: pages on file: origins get it when they commit, and
loadDataWithBaseURL with a non-data base URL grants it explicitly in
AwContents.loadUrl. Actually loading the special file URLs (and the
allowFileAccess exemption for them) happens later in
AwProxyingURLLoaderFactory, so that is unaffected.

The difference is that special file URLs are no longer a special carve
out. They go through the same browser side permission check as every
other file URL.

parent
8484f6e94f362a06c52b2604e3164229377e7a88 [ diff ] 
Capture SidePanelEntryKey by value in ShowSidePanelForKey

ShowSidePanelForKey in PinnedSidePanelInteractiveTest took
SidePanelEntryKey by value and captured it by reference ([&]) in the
deferred Do(...) step callback. When the step executed later, `key` was
a dangling stack reference, causing SidePanelUIBase::Show to fail
CHECK(unique_key.has_value()) in SwitchBetweenDifferentEntries and
PinActiveItemHighlightsButton.

Capture `this` and `key` by value in the Do(...) callback.

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Ainteractive_ui_tests%21gtest%3A%3APinnedSidePanelInteractiveTest%23SwitchBetweenDifferentEntries

[contextual_tasks] Allow side panel permission prompts with Lens Overlay

ShouldIgnorePermissionRequest() dropped every permission prompt while
the Lens Overlay was showing on the active tab, including prompts from
other WebContents like the Contextual Tasks side panel. As a result, the
microphone prompt for voice search in the side panel never showed in
Lens search mode.

Only suppress prompts from the page under the overlay/other pages, i.e.
the active tab's WebContents, but not the lens panel (or anything with a
location bar override).

screencast/cast/NDc2Njc3NjI0NzE4OTUwNHwzYTcxYjRlMC0yOQ

Move DefaultBrowserManager initialization from GlobalFeatures::PostBrowserProcessInitCore() to PostBrowserProcessInit() and reset it in PostMainMessageLoopRun() so registry monitoring and shell checks do not run in OnTestStart() before a TaskEnvironment exists.

ba292f9ced0e8ccdcf465d0e22d546b4b5b87ed3
parent
62449b6f478dc9e021a3297c1441b26a85889b0d [ diff ] 
glic: Add kGlicShowLoaderUntilMainPageOnload param to kGlicSsr.

By default, `features::kGlicSsr` bypasses the Glic loading panel
immediately, which can expose a blank panel on slow networks while the
initial guest HTML downloads. Waiting for the web client ready
handshake, however, hides the server-rendered input area until client
JavaScript finishes bootstrapping.

Add `features::kGlicShowLoaderUntilMainPageOnload`
("show_loader_until_main_page_onload", disabled by default) as a boolean
FeatureParam on `features::kGlicSsr`. When enabled alongside `kGlicSsr`,
`GlicWebClientManager` notifies its delegate on
`DocumentOnLoadCompletedInPrimaryMainFrame` for regular API-allowed
guest pages, keeping the loading panel visible only until the guest main
page completes its `onload` event.

In `<webview>` mode, `GlicPreloadHandler::OnGuestMainPageLoaded`
notifies `GlicAppController`, which transitions to
`WebUiState::kGuestLoaded` and displays `#guestPanel` while waiting for
the web client to initialize or time out. In NoWebview mode,
`GlicNoWebviewContentsManager` tracks `guest_main_page_loaded_` in an
`ObservableValue` observed by `OverlayContentsManager`.

Bug: b:571196676
Fixed: b:574092807
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*ShowLoaderUntilMainPageOnload*

tree
d6e852d55da65070fd0f110ece433b6df252e289
parent
ec97530de940b3ed17180908ed0c22b05829b722 [ diff ] 
[WebUI omnibox] Focus the WebUI omnibox once in LocationBarView

In Full WebUI mode `LocationBarView` is the focusable Views proxy for the
WebUI omnibox. `FocusLocation()` routed Views focus to it with
`FocusManager::SetFocusedView(this)`, which synchronously fires
`OnFocus()`. `OnFocus()` then focused the WebUI omnibox twice with a
guessed intent (`omnibox_view_->SetFocus(false)`, which delegates to the
popup view in this mode, and `popup_view->OnFocus(false)`), after which
`FocusLocation()` did it a third time with the real intent. The same
pattern was in `OnFullWebUiOmniboxReady()`.

Make `FocusLocation()` and `OnFullWebUiOmniboxReady()` focus the WebUI
omnibox once, with the caller's intent, instead of up to three times.
Callers suppress `OnFocus()` with `moving_focus_to_webui_` while calling
`SetFocusedView(this)` and then call `ForwardFocusToWebUI()` directly;
`OnFocus()` only forwards focus when `FocusManager` moves focus on its own
(tab traversal, focus restoration). In all cases regaining focus on an
unfocused omnibox selects all, like native `views::Textfield`, and a
user-initiated request selects all unconditionally and queries Zero-Prefix
Suggestions, like native `OmniboxViewViews::SetFocus()`.

`LocationBarView` no longer routes Full WebUI focus through
`OmniboxViewViews::SetFocus()`.

Bug: b:562737541

WebUIExtensionsContainer: Fix UserHostRestrictionsWithPermittedSitesBT

Fix UserHostRestrictionsWithPermittedSitesBrowserTest.UserPermittedSites
with WebUIExtensionsContainer enabled.

When kExtensionsMenuAccessControl is disabled (the /0 parameterization
of the test), ExtensionActionViewModel paints a "blocked action"
decoration on the extension icon: a 12dp-radius circle centered in the
icon area. IconWithBadgeImageSource::PaintBlockedActionDecoration()
CHECKed that this 24dp circle fits within the icon area, which holds for
the 28dp Views toolbar button but not for the 20dp icons requested by
WebUIToolbarExtensionsContainer, causing a fatal check failure
(icon_with_badge_image_source.cc: "24 vs. 20") whenever an extension's
script injection was blocked on the active tab.

Clamp the decoration radius to half the icon area size instead of
CHECKing, so the decoration scales down for smaller icons.

[3P DSE NTP on AL] Add Prepopulated MVTs to 3P DSE WebUI NTP to AL

This change introduces prepopulated MVTs such as the Chrome Web Store
(CWS) to the 3P DSE WebUI NTP on Android Desktop.

This is done through the top_sites_factory, as they were originally
compiled out for Android platforms. Additionally, to prevent this change
from impacting external behavior outside of Chrome, the prepopulated
pages are filtered out from the
auxiliary_search_top_site_provider_bridge.

The changes are gated behind the UseWebUiNtp3PDSE Chrome feature flag,
and are only impacting the remote 3P DSE WebUI NTP, and not the already
launched 1P DSE WebUI NTP. New unit tests were added to cover the new
logic.

Screencast After (3P DSE w/CWS):
screencast/cast/NjMxNDY1ODYzNzAyMTE4NHw4YjJiZWRmZS0wZQ

Note on the screencast the lingering MVTs on runtime DSE change.

parent
a96c702e8b78f862544206e10b05d7d15407f910 [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Highlight side panel permission chips in their context

The permission prompt bubble for the Contextual Tasks side panel is
anchored to the WebUI request chip, but the highlight relied on the
anchor: GetChipAnchor() couldn't set a highlighted element because
BubbleDialogDelegate::SetHighlightedElement() resolves IDs in the
anchor widget's (browser window's) context, where it would find the
main toolbar's chip instead. The indicator chip's bubbles (content
setting bubble and page info) have the same problem: clicking the side
panel's indicator chip highlights the main toolbar's indicator chip if
that one is showing.

Add a SetHighlightedElement() overload that takes an explicit
ui::ElementContext, plumb it through
AnchorConfiguration::highlighted_element_context, and have
ContextualTasksLocationBar::GetChipAnchor() highlight the request chip
in the toolbar WebUI's context. PermissionDashboardController now
highlights the indicator chip in the location bar's element context
when it has one. Highlighting by ID also re-applies the highlight if
the WebUI re-registers the chip while the bubble is open.

follow-up CLs. Uses `WebContentsUserData` to store per-tab attribution
state, and `WebContentsObserver` to keep state accurate across tab
lifecycle events.

SECURITY.md guidelines - clarify Rule of Two checklist item

Update the checklist to ask whether the component complies with the Rule
of Two and, if so, how, rather than assuming compliance is always
required or applicable.

I was using an agent to help write a SECURITY.md file and it was trying
to shoehorn something that's not really in compliance into a rationale
that it was. Let's be clear that we want to document the current state,
not assume / force it into a box that may not it.

[Extensions] Fix tabs.move to WINDOW_ID_CURRENT within the tab's window

MoveTab compared WINDOW_ID_CURRENT with the tab's window ID before
resolving it, so moving a tab to its own window took the cross-window
path. For a window's only tab this crashed in Browser::OnTabInsertedAt.

parent
8c6253b6d9ab56548be68e354a09de2e1410747d [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Remove last base::Passed() usage

In WebRequestProxyingURLLoaderFactory,
InProgressRequest::ContinueAuthRequest() still used
base::BindRepeating() with base::Passed(&callback) to bind the move-only
WebRequestAPI::AuthRequestCallback, solely because the continuation is
either handed to OnAuthRequired() (which takes a base::OnceCallback) for
blocking listeners, or run synchronously when OnAuthRequired() returns
AUTH_REQUIRED_RESPONSE_NO_ACTION.

Split a base::BindOnce() continuation with base::SplitOnceCallback()
instead, matching HandleResponseOrRedirectHeaders() in the same class
and eliminating the last remaining base::Passed() call in //extensions.

No behavior change.

tree
b7b21440e7830acdc431f4d360a3fa1d9dba278c
parent
390e6c4848fa33e6aad27cd26923d331bffa9476 [ diff ] 
Remove void element end tags from HTML WPTs

Some elements (such as img, input, link, meta, embed, source, etc) have
no end tag. Writing their end tag anyway is a parse error, and the
parser drops the tag, so the source suggests a structure the document
never had. This CL removes these end tags from WPT tests in
third_party/blink/web_tests/external/wpt/html.

d2323ed8d22cf5145e5c758d9a4af140853d052e [ diff ] 
[WebUI Toolbar] Test omnibox state after dragging text out

Add tests that check the WebUI omnibox state after a drag out of it
ends, to match the native omnibox:
- The omnibox text is unchanged, whether the drop target copies or
moves the text, or the drag is cancelled.
- Typed text stays in edit mode rather than reverting to the current
page's URL.
- The omnibox still accepts text dropped into it from outside, i.e. it
no longer treats drags as its own once the drag out has ended.

[Persist] Filter canonical collections without divergent entries on load

To accommodate taking structural snapshots during restoration,
divergent_nodes is populated by crawling the collections currently
present in memory at snapshot time rather than via incremental
move/remove callbacks.

Update ReconcileDivergentNodes() to also filter all_divergent_children
and deleted_nodes out of canonical collections that lack their own entry
in divergent_children_map_, preventing moved or deleted tabs from being
duplicated or resurrected on the next load.

LHS: Re-enable NewTabPageAppTest.LeftHandSide on Desktop Android

crrev.com/c/8585936 filtered this test because "realbox secondary side
uses the RHS width when feature enabled" failed on the Brya bots. At
fractional device scale factors, ResizeObserver's contentRect reports a
900px #rhsWrapper as 899.984375px, so the old contentRect.width >= 900
check was false.

crrev.com/c/8575478 switched that check to clientWidth, which is 900.
The @container rhsWrapper (min-width: 900px) rule matches either way
because Blink allows one LayoutUnit of error when comparing container
sizes.

Verified on Mac with browser_tests --force-device-scale-factor=1.2,
1.333333 and 2.4: the realbox test passes at ToT and fails with "expected
false to be true" if the check is reverted to contentRect.width.

7ca2e189946e5bd60cff178afacafa65e8cae800 [ diff ] 
[WebInstallAPI] Enforce JSON content type on fetched manifests

Update the API's manifest fetcher to read the MIME type from the HTTP
response headers, and accept only JSON MIME types (application/json,
application/manifest+json, etc). This aligns with the spec [1]

The fetcher's response fails with kInvalidContentType if its headers are
missing. Or, if its Content-Type is missing, empty or not JSON.
Intentionally ignore any MIME type sniffed from the body.

Web pages still see the same generic DataError as before. No new
information is exposed.

Temporarily fire our preexisting devtools issue for
ManifestParsingOrNetworkError until a custom string is added in
crbug.com/575536637.

[1]
https://wicg.github.io/install-element/index.html#fetching-an-install-manifest

Bug: 562035733,333795265,575536637

833e3ce89840447d0f1bbfd6bf0bb9d5c72d8e13 [ diff ] 
[X11] Keep the full keycode range after mapping changes

A keyboard mapping update can describe a change to just one key.
Chromium currently treats that update as the keyboard's full keycode
range. This can drop mappings for the other keys when refreshing the
core X11 keyboard state. Modifier updates can also overwrite the range
with undefined values.

This CL:
- Keep the full keycode range when refreshing keyboard mappings.
- Add tests for partial keyboard updates and modifier updates, and
verify that XKB range changes still work.

Re-enable ConnectionTest unit tests on Mac and all platforms

Re-enable the 6 ConnectionTest unit tests that were disabled on Mac
(Disconnect, Control, Events, Video, VideoWithSlowSignaling, and
DestroyOnIncomingMessage) and the 3 video tests that were disabled on
all platforms (VideoStats, FirstCaptureFailed, and SecondCaptureFailed).

Pass webrtc::kFullDesktopScreenId instead of 0 to StartVideoStream() in
ConnectionTest so the stream name ('screen_stream') matches the label
used by WebrtcConnectionToClient's video_stats_dispatcher_ and
WebrtcConnectionToHost's WebrtcVideoRendererAdapter.

AW: Route CTS test bugs to the WebView component

When a CTS test fails or flakes, LUCI Analysis cannot map it to the
WebView component because the test runner does not provide a file
location for CTS tests, leading to fallback routing to the root Chromium
component.

This CL introduces a --test-location-for-bug-routing flag in the test
runner, and modifies run_cts.py to pass the WebView DIR_METADATA path.
This ensures ResultDB accurately reports a location within the
//android_webview tree, allowing LUCI Analysis to route CTS test
failures to the WebView bugcop queue.

Test: build/android/test_runner.py junit --help

97c8ef0ec5eefeb2b1a0b8ae516c6373b50120ed
parent
c229f168977e78a40b7cc4ff7586282c686a2d8a [ diff ] 
[WebUI Toolbar] Make clicking an overflowed Avatar button work.

Before this CL, the WebUI Avatar button was wired up to overflow and
appear on the overflow menu when there's no space for it and the WebUI
toolbar is enabled, but clicking the button did nothing. This CL fixes
that.

This CL introduces WebUIAvatarToolbarButton::RunWhenVisible(). It takes
a callback and increments a counter which, when non-zero, prevents the
Avatar button from overflowing. Then it waits until the Avatar button is
visible, and once that happens (which may already be the case), it
invokes the callback, passing in a ScopedClosureRunner which, when
deleted, decrements the previously incremented counter, which, when it
becomes zero, results in allowing the avatar button to overflow once
more.

WebUIAvatarToolbarButton::ButtonPressed() now calls RunWhenVisible(),
and passes the ScopedClosureRunner it gets to
AvatarToolbarButtonStateManager::HandleButtonPressed(), which is now
responsible for keeping the ScopedClosureRunner around until any bubble
it shows has closed.

Note that this affects callers of ButtonPressed() other than just the
overflow menu - accelerators, for instance, will also now show the
Profile menu if hidden. This is a deliberate change, and should improve
functionality.

There are other calls that show bubbles anchored to the Avatar button
that we likely want to wire up to RunWhenVisible() directly. For
example, there are incognito-related dialogs that anchor to the profile
button through IncognitoClearBrowsingDataDialogCoordinator() that should
probably use it. Migrating those is left for future CLs.
